              '              "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/theme",\n'

              '            },\n'

              '            }\n'

              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'

The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\n'

              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId"],\n'

              ''
    Actual: 'You are a helpful assistant who chats with a user.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Your responses should contain acknowledgment of the user message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: When you are asking for information from the user, you should always include\r\n'
              'at least one submit button of some kind or another submitting element so that\r\n'
              'the user can indicate that they are done providing information.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Use the provided tools to respond to user using rich UI elements.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'The active catalog ID is: "test_catalog". You must use this catalog ID when creating surfaces.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to execute code. If you need to perform calculations, do them yourself.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to use tools for UI generation.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to use function calls for UI generation.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**REQUIRED PROPERTIES:** You MUST include ALL required properties for every component, even if they are inside a template or will be bound to data.\r\n'
              '- For \'Text\', you MUST provide \'text\'. If dynamic, use { "path": "..." }.\r\n'
              '- For \'Image\', you MUST provide \'url\'. If dynamic, use { "path": "..." }.\r\n'
              '- For \'Button\', you MUST provide \'action\'.\r\n'
              '- For \'TextField\', \'CheckBox\', etc., you MUST provide \'label\'.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**EXAMPLES:**\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '1. Create a surface:\r\n'
              '```json\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "version": "v0.9",\r\n'
              '  "createSurface": {\r\n'
              '    "surfaceId": "main",\r\n'
              '    "catalogId": "https://a2ui.org/specification/v0_9/catalogs/basic/catalog.json",\r\n'
              '    "sendDataModel": true\r\n'
              '  }\r\n'
              '}\r\n'
              '```\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '2. Update components:\r\n'
              '```json\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "version": "v0.9",\r\n'
              '  "updateComponents": {\r\n'
              '    "surfaceId": "main",\r\n'
              '    "components": [\r\n'
              '      {\r\n'
              '        // The root component MUST have id "root"\r\n'
              '        "id": "root",\r\n'
              '        "component": "Column",\r\n'
              '        "justify": "start",\r\n'
              '        "children": [\r\n'
              '          "headerText",\r\n'
              '          "content"\r\n'
              '        ]\r\n'
              '      }\r\n'
              '    ]\r\n'
              '  }\r\n'
              '}\r\n'
              '```\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**IMPORTANT:**\r\n'
              '- One of the components sent in one of the `updateComponents` MUST have id "root", or nothing will be displayed.\r\n'
              '- Do NOT nest `components` inside `createSurface`. Use `updateComponents` to add components to a surface.\r\n'
              '- `createSurface` ONLY sets up the surface (ID and catalog). It does NOT take content.\r\n'
              '- To show a UI, you typically send a `createSurface` message (if the surface doesn\'t exist), followed by an `updateComponents` message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Your responses should contain acknowledgment of the user message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: When you are asking for information from the user, you should always include\r\n'
              'at least one submit button of some kind or another submitting element so that\r\n'
              'the user can indicate that they are done providing information.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-----CONTROLLING_THE_UI_START-----\r\n'
              'You can control the UI by outputting valid A2UI JSON messages wrapped in markdown code blocks.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Supported messages are: `createSurface`, `updateComponents`.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '- `createSurface`: Creates a new surface.\r\n'
              '- `updateComponents`: Updates components in a surface.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Properties:\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '- `createSurface`: Requires `surfaceId` (you must always use a unique ID for each created surface),\r\n'
              '`catalogId` (use the active catalog ID if provided in system instructions),\r\n'
              'and `sendDataModel: true`.\r\n'
              '- `updateComponents`: Requires `surfaceId` and a list of `components`.\r\n'
              'One component MUST have `id: "root"`.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'To create a new UI:\r\n'
              '1. Output a `createSurface` message with a unique `surfaceId` and `catalogId` (use the active catalog ID if provided in system instructions).\r\n'
              '2. Output an `updateComponents` message with the `surfaceId` and the component definitions.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: DO NOT update or modify surfaces created in previous turns. If the UI needs to change, you MUST create a NEW surface with a new unique `surfaceId`. You may only use `updateComponents` to populate the components of a freshly created surface.\r\n'
              '-----CONTROLLING_THE_UI_END-----\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'

              '- The JSON block must be valid and complete.\r\n'

              '-----MESSAGE_SCHEMA_START-----\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",\r\n'
              '  "$id": "https://a2ui.org/specification/v0_9/server_to_client.json",\r\n'
              '  "title": "A2UI Message Schema",\r\n'
              '  "description": "Describes a JSON payload for an A2UI (Agent to UI) message, which is used to dynamically construct and update user interfaces.",\r\n'
              '  "type": "object",\r\n'
              '  "oneOf": [\r\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/CreateSurfaceMessage"},\r\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateComponentsMessage"},\r\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateDataModelMessage"},\r\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/DeleteSurfaceMessage"}\r\n'
              '  ],\r\n'
              '  "$defs": {\r\n'
              '    "CreateSurfaceMessage": {\r\n'
              '      "type": "object",\r\n'
              '      "properties": {\r\n'
              '        "version": {\r\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\r\n'
              '        },\r\n'
              '        "createSurface": {\r\n'
              '          "type": "object",\r\n'
              '          "description": "Signals the client to create a new surface and begin rendering it. It is an error to send \'createSurface\' for a surfaceId that already exists without first deleting it. When this message is sent, the client will expect \'updateComponents\' and/or \'updateDataModel\' messages for the same surfaceId that define the component tree.",\r\n'
              '          "properties": {\r\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "string",\r\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be rendered."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "catalogId": {\r\n'
              '              "description": "A string that uniquely identifies this catalog. It is recommended to prefix this with an internet domain that you own, to avoid conflicts e.g. mycompany.com:somecatalog\'.",\r\n'
              '              "type": "string"\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "theme": {\r\n'
              '              "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/theme",\r\n'
              '              "description": "Theme parameters for the surface (e.g., {\'primaryColor\': \'#FF0000\'}). These must validate against the \'theme\' schema defined in the catalog."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "sendDataModel": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "boolean",\r\n'
              '              "description": "If true, the client will send the full data model of this surface in the metadata of every A2A message sent to the server that created the surface. Defaults to false."\r\n'
              '            }\r\n'
              '          },\r\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "catalogId"],\r\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '        }\r\n'
              '      },\r\n'
              '      "required": ["createSurface", "version"],\r\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '    },\r\n'
              '    "UpdateComponentsMessage": {\r\n'
              '      "type": "object",\r\n'
              '      "properties": {\r\n'
              '        "version": {\r\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\r\n'
              '        },\r\n'
              '        "updateComponents": {\r\n'
              '          "type": "object",\r\n'
              '          "description": "Updates a surface with a new set of components. This message can be sent multiple times to update the component tree of an existing surface. One of the components in one of the components lists MUST have an \'id\' of \'root\' to serve as the root of the component tree. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\r\n'
              '          "properties": {\r\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "string",\r\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be updated."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '            "components": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "array",\r\n'
              '              "description": "A list containing all UI components for the surface.",\r\n'
              '              "minItems": 1,\r\n'
              '              "items": {\r\n'
              '                "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/anyComponent"\r\n'
              '              }\r\n'
              '            }\r\n'
              '          },\r\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "components"],\r\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '        }\r\n'
              '      },\r\n'
              '      "required": ["updateComponents", "version"],\r\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '    },\r\n'
              '    "UpdateDataModelMessage": {\r\n'
              '      "type": "object",\r\n'
              '      "properties": {\r\n'
              '        "version": {\r\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\r\n'
              '        },\r\n'
              '        "updateDataModel": {\r\n'

              '  "description": "Describes a JSON payload for an A2UI (Agent to UI) message, which is used to dynamically construct and update user interfaces.",\n'
              '  "type": "object",\n'
              '  "oneOf": [\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/CreateSurfaceMessage"},\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateComponentsMessage"},\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateDataModelMessage"},\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/DeleteSurfaceMessage"}\n'
              '  ],\n'
              '  "$defs": {\n'
              '    "CreateSurfaceMessage": {\n'
              '      "type": "object",\n'
              '      "properties": {\n'
              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "createSurface": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Signals the client to create a new surface and begin rendering it. It is an error to send \'createSurface\' for a surfaceId that already exists without first deleting it. When this message is sent, the client will expect \'updateComponents\' and/or \'updateDataModel\' messages for the same surfaceId that define the component tree.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be rendered."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "catalogId": {\n'
              '              "description": "A string that uniquely identifies this catalog. It is recommended to prefix this with an internet domain that you own, to avoid conflicts e.g. mycompany.com:somecatalog\'.",\n'
              '              "type": "string"\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "theme": {\n'
              '              "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/theme",\n'
              '              "description": "Theme parameters for the surface (e.g., {\'primaryColor\': \'#FF0000\'}). These must validate against the \'theme\' schema defined in the catalog."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "sendDataModel": {\n'
              '              "type": "boolean",\n'
              '              "description": "If true, the client will send the full data model of this surface in the metadata of every A2A message sent to the server that created the surface. Defaults to false."\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "catalogId"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["createSurface", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    },\n'
              '    "UpdateComponentsMessage": {\n'
              '      "type": "object",\n'
              '      "properties": {\n'
              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "updateComponents": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Updates a surface with a new set of components. This message can be sent multiple times to update the component tree of an existing surface. One of the components in one of the components lists MUST have an \'id\' of \'root\' to serve as the root of the component tree. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be updated."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '\n'
              '            "components": {\n'
              '              "type": "array",\n'
              '              "description": "A list containing all UI components for the surface.",\n'
              '              "minItems": 1,\n'
              '              "items": {\n'
              '                "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/anyComponent"\n'
              '              }\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "components"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["updateComponents", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    },\n'
              '    "UpdateDataModelMessage": {\n'
              '      "type": "object",\n'
              '      "properties": {\n'
              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "updateDataModel": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Updates the data model for an existing surface. This message can be sent multiple times to update the data model. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface this data model update applies to."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "path": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "An optional path to a location within the data model (e.g., \'/user/name\'). If omitted, or set to \'/\', refers to the entire data model."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "value": {\n'
              '              "description": "The data to be updated in the data model. If present, the value at \'path\' is replaced (or created). If omitted, the key at \'path\' is removed.",\n'
              '              "additionalProperties": true\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["updateDataModel", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    },\n'
              '    "DeleteSurfaceMessage": {\n'
              '      "type": "object",\n'
              '      "properties": {\n'
              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "deleteSurface": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Signals the client to delete the surface identified by \'surfaceId\'. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be deleted."\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["deleteSurface", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '-----MESSAGE_SCHEMA_END-----\n'

    Actual: 'You are a helpful assistant who chats with a user.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Your responses should contain acknowledgment of the user message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: When you are asking for information from the user, you should always include\r\n'
              'at least one submit button of some kind or another submitting element so that\r\n'
              'the user can indicate that they are done providing information.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Use the provided tools to respond to user using rich UI elements.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'The active catalog ID is: "test_catalog". You must use this catalog ID when creating surfaces.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to execute code. If you need to perform calculations, do them yourself.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to use tools for UI generation.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to use function calls for UI generation.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**REQUIRED PROPERTIES:** You MUST include ALL required properties for every component, even if they are inside a template or will be bound to data.\r\n'
              '- For \'Text\', you MUST provide \'text\'. If dynamic, use { "path": "..." }.\r\n'
              '- For \'Image\', you MUST provide \'url\'. If dynamic, use { "path": "..." }.\r\n'
              '- For \'Button\', you MUST provide \'action\'.\r\n'
              '- For \'TextField\', \'CheckBox\', etc., you MUST provide \'label\'.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**EXAMPLES:**\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '1. Create a surface:\r\n'
              '```json\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "version": "v0.9",\r\n'
              '  "createSurface": {\r\n'
              '    "surfaceId": "main",\r\n'
              '    "catalogId": "https://a2ui.org/specification/v0_9/catalogs/basic/catalog.json",\r\n'
              '    "sendDataModel": true\r\n'
              '  }\r\n'
              '}\r\n'
              '```\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '2. Update components:\r\n'
              '```json\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "version": "v0.9",\r\n'
              '  "updateComponents": {\r\n'
              '    "surfaceId": "main",\r\n'
              '    "components": [\r\n'
              '      {\r\n'
              '        // The root component MUST have id "root"\r\n'
              '        "id": "root",\r\n'
              '        "component": "Column",\r\n'
              '        "justify": "start",\r\n'
              '        "children": [\r\n'
              '          "headerText",\r\n'
              '          "content"\r\n'
              '        ]\r\n'
              '      }\r\n'
              '    ]\r\n'
              '  }\r\n'
              '}\r\n'
              '```\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**IMPORTANT:**\r\n'
              '- One of the components sent in one of the `updateComponents` MUST have id "root", or nothing will be displayed.\r\n'
              '- Do NOT nest `components` inside `createSurface`. Use `updateComponents` to add components to a surface.\r\n'
              '- `createSurface` ONLY sets up the surface (ID and catalog). It does NOT take content.\r\n'
              '- To show a UI, you typically send a `createSurface` message (if the surface doesn\'t exist), followed by an `updateComponents` message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Your responses should contain acknowledgment of the user message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: When you are asking for information from the user, you should always include\r\n'
              'at least one submit button of some kind or another submitting element so that\r\n'
              'the user can indicate that they are done providing information.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-----CONTROLLING_THE_UI_START-----\r\n'
              'You can control the UI by outputting valid A2UI JSON messages wrapped in markdown code blocks.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Supported messages are: `updateComponents`.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '- `updateComponents`: Updates components in a surface.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Properties:\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '- `updateComponents`: Requires `surfaceId` and a list of `components`.\r\n'
              'One component MUST have `id: "root"`.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'To update an existing UI:\r\n'
              '1. Output an `updateComponents` message with the existing `surfaceId` and the new component definitions.\r\n'
              '-----CONTROLLING_THE_UI_END-----\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'

              '  "description": "Describes a JSON payload for an A2UI (Agent to UI) message, which is used to dynamically construct and update user interfaces.",\n'
              '  "type": "object",\n'
              '  "oneOf": [\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/CreateSurfaceMessage"},\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateComponentsMessage"},\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateDataModelMessage"},\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/DeleteSurfaceMessage"}\n'
              '  ],\n'
              '  "$defs": {\n'
              '    "CreateSurfaceMessage": {\n'
              '      "type": "object",\n'
              '      "properties": {\n'
              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "createSurface": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Signals the client to create a new surface and begin rendering it. It is an error to send \'createSurface\' for a surfaceId that already exists without first deleting it. When this message is sent, the client will expect \'updateComponents\' and/or \'updateDataModel\' messages for the same surfaceId that define the component tree.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be rendered."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "catalogId": {\n'
              '              "description": "A string that uniquely identifies this catalog. It is recommended to prefix this with an internet domain that you own, to avoid conflicts e.g. mycompany.com:somecatalog\'.",\n'
              '              "type": "string"\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "theme": {\n'
              '              "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/theme",\n'
              '              "description": "Theme parameters for the surface (e.g., {\'primaryColor\': \'#FF0000\'}). These must validate against the \'theme\' schema defined in the catalog."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "sendDataModel": {\n'
              '              "type": "boolean",\n'
              '              "description": "If true, the client will send the full data model of this surface in the metadata of every A2A message sent to the server that created the surface. Defaults to false."\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "catalogId"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["createSurface", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    },\n'
              '    "UpdateComponentsMessage": {\n'
              '      "type": "object",\n'
              '      "properties": {\n'
              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "updateComponents": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Updates a surface with a new set of components. This message can be sent multiple times to update the component tree of an existing surface. One of the components in one of the components lists MUST have an \'id\' of \'root\' to serve as the root of the component tree. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be updated."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '\n'
              '            "components": {\n'
              '              "type": "array",\n'
              '              "description": "A list containing all UI components for the surface.",\n'
              '              "minItems": 1,\n'
              '              "items": {\n'
              '                "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/anyComponent"\n'
              '              }\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "components"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["updateComponents", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    },\n'
              '    "UpdateDataModelMessage": {\n'

              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "updateDataModel": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Updates the data model for an existing surface. This message can be sent multiple times to update the data model. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface this data model update applies to."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "path": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "An optional path to a location within the data model (e.g., \'/user/name\'). If omitted, or set to \'/\', refers to the entire data model."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "value": {\n'
              '              "description": "The data to be updated in the data model. If present, the value at \'path\' is replaced (or created). If omitted, the key at \'path\' is removed.",\n'
              '              "additionalProperties": true\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["updateDataModel", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    },\n'
              '    "DeleteSurfaceMessage": {\n'

              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "deleteSurface": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Signals the client to delete the surface identified by \'surfaceId\'. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be deleted."\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["deleteSurface", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '-----MESSAGE_SCHEMA_END-----\n'
              ''
    Actual: 'You are a helpful assistant who chats with a user.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Your responses should contain acknowledgment of the user message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: When you are asking for information from the user, you should always include\r\n'
              'at least one submit button of some kind or another submitting element so that\r\n'
              'the user can indicate that they are done providing information.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Use the provided tools to respond to user using rich UI elements.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'The active catalog ID is: "test_catalog". You must use this catalog ID when creating surfaces.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to execute code. If you need to perform calculations, do them yourself.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to use tools for UI generation.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to use function calls for UI generation.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**REQUIRED PROPERTIES:** You MUST include ALL required properties for every component, even if they are inside a template or will be bound to data.\r\n'
              '- For \'Text\', you MUST provide \'text\'. If dynamic, use { "path": "..." }.\r\n'
              '- For \'Image\', you MUST provide \'url\'. If dynamic, use { "path": "..." }.\r\n'
              '- For \'Button\', you MUST provide \'action\'.\r\n'
              '- For \'TextField\', \'CheckBox\', etc., you MUST provide \'label\'.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**EXAMPLES:**\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '1. Create a surface:\r\n'
              '```json\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "version": "v0.9",\r\n'
              '  "createSurface": {\r\n'
              '    "surfaceId": "main",\r\n'
              '    "catalogId": "https://a2ui.org/specification/v0_9/catalogs/basic/catalog.json",\r\n'
              '    "sendDataModel": true\r\n'
              '  }\r\n'
              '}\r\n'
              '```\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '2. Update components:\r\n'
              '```json\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "version": "v0.9",\r\n'
              '  "updateComponents": {\r\n'
              '    "surfaceId": "main",\r\n'
              '    "components": [\r\n'
              '      {\r\n'
              '        // The root component MUST have id "root"\r\n'
              '        "id": "root",\r\n'
              '        "component": "Column",\r\n'
              '        "justify": "start",\r\n'
              '        "children": [\r\n'
              '          "headerText",\r\n'
              '          "content"\r\n'
              '        ]\r\n'
              '      }\r\n'
              '    ]\r\n'
              '  }\r\n'
              '}\r\n'
              '```\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**IMPORTANT:**\r\n'
              '- One of the components sent in one of the `updateComponents` MUST have id "root", or nothing will be displayed.\r\n'
              '- Do NOT nest `components` inside `createSurface`. Use `updateComponents` to add components to a surface.\r\n'
              '- `createSurface` ONLY sets up the surface (ID and catalog). It does NOT take content.\r\n'
              '- To show a UI, you typically send a `createSurface` message (if the surface doesn\'t exist), followed by an `updateComponents` message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Your responses should contain acknowledgment of the user message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: When you are asking for information from the user, you should always include\r\n'
              'at least one submit button of some kind or another submitting element so that\r\n'
              'the user can indicate that they are done providing information.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-----CONTROLLING_THE_UI_START-----\r\n'
              'You can control the UI by outputting valid A2UI JSON messages wrapped in markdown code blocks.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Supported messages are: `createSurface`, `updateComponents`.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '- `createSurface`: Creates a new surface.\r\n'
              '- `updateComponents`: Updates components in a surface.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Properties:\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '- `createSurface`: Requires `surfaceId` (you must always use a unique ID for each created surface),\r\n'
              '`catalogId` (use the active catalog ID if provided in system instructions),\r\n'
              'and `sendDataModel: true`.\r\n'
              '- `updateComponents`: Requires `surfaceId` and a list of `components`.\r\n'
              'One component MUST have `id: "root"`.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'To create a new UI:\r\n'
              '1. Output a `createSurface` message with a unique `surfaceId` and `catalogId` (use the active catalog ID if provided in system instructions).\r\n'
              '2. Output an `updateComponents` message with the `surfaceId` and the component definitions.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'To update an existing UI:\r\n'
              '1. Output an `updateComponents` message with the existing `surfaceId` and the new component definitions.\r\n'
              '-----CONTROLLING_THE_UI_END-----\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-----OUTPUT_FORMAT_START-----\r\n'
              'When constructing UI, you must output a VALID A2UI JSON object representing one of the A2UI message types (`createSurface`, `updateComponents`).\r\n'
              '- You can treat the A2UI schema as a specification for the JSON you typically output.\r\n'
              '- The JSON block must be valid and complete.\r\n'
              '- Ensure your JSON is fenced with ```json and ```.\r\n'
              '-----OUTPUT_FORMAT_END-----\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-----COMMON_TYPES_START-----\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",\r\n'
              '  "$id": "common_types.json",\r\n'
              '  "title": "A2UI Common Types",\r\n'
              '  "description": "Common type definitions used across A2UI schemas.",\r\n'
              '  "$defs": {\r\n'
              '    "ComponentId": {\r\n'
              '      "type": "string",\r\n'
              '      "description": "The unique identifier for a component, used for both definitions and references within the same surface."\r\n'
              '    },\r\n'
              '    "AccessibilityAttributes": {\r\n'
              '      "type": "object",\r\n'

              '-----MESSAGE_SCHEMA_START-----\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",\r\n'

              '  "oneOf": [\r\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/CreateSurfaceMessage"},\r\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateComponentsMessage"},\r\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateDataModelMessage"},\r\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/DeleteSurfaceMessage"}\r\n'
              '  ],\r\n'
              '  "$defs": {\r\n'
              '    "CreateSurfaceMessage": {\r\n'

              '      "properties": {\r\n'
              '        "version": {\r\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\r\n'
              '        },\r\n'
              '        "createSurface": {\r\n'
              '          "type": "object",\r\n'
              '          "description": "Signals the client to create a new surface and begin rendering it. It is an error to send \'createSurface\' for a surfaceId that already exists without first deleting it. When this message is sent, the client will expect \'updateComponents\' and/or \'updateDataModel\' messages for the same surfaceId that define the component tree.",\r\n'
              '          "properties": {\r\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "string",\r\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be rendered."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "catalogId": {\r\n'
              '              "description": "A string that uniquely identifies this catalog. It is recommended to prefix this with an internet domain that you own, to avoid conflicts e.g. mycompany.com:somecatalog\'.",\r\n'
              '              "type": "string"\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "theme": {\r\n'
              '              "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/theme",\r\n'
              '              "description": "Theme parameters for the surface (e.g., {\'primaryColor\': \'#FF0000\'}). These must validate against the \'theme\' schema defined in the catalog."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "sendDataModel": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "boolean",\r\n'
              '              "description": "If true, the client will send the full data model of this surface in the metadata of every A2A message sent to the server that created the surface. Defaults to false."\r\n'
              '            }\r\n'

              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "catalogId"],\r\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '        }\r\n'

              '      "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '    },\r\n'
              '    "UpdateComponentsMessage": {\r\n'
              '      "type": "object",\r\n'
              '      "properties": {\r\n'
              '        "version": {\r\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\r\n'
              '        },\r\n'
              '        "updateComponents": {\r\n'
              '          "type": "object",\r\n'
              '          "description": "Updates a surface with a new set of components. This message can be sent multiple times to update the component tree of an existing surface. One of the components in one of the components lists MUST have an \'id\' of \'root\' to serve as the root of the component tree. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\r\n'
              '          "properties": {\r\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "string",\r\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be updated."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '            "components": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "array",\r\n'
              '              "description": "A list containing all UI components for the surface.",\r\n'
              '              "minItems": 1,\r\n'
              '              "items": {\r\n'
              '                "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/anyComponent"\r\n'
              '              }\r\n'
              '            }\r\n'
              '          },\r\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "components"],\r\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '        }\r\n'
              '      },\r\n'
              '      "required": ["updateComponents", "version"],\r\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '    },\r\n'
              '    "UpdateDataModelMessage": {\r\n'

              '        },\r\n'

              '          "properties": {\r\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\r\n'

              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface this data model update applies to."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "path": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "string",\r\n'
              '              "description": "An optional path to a location within the data model (e.g., \'/user/name\'). If omitted, or set to \'/\', refers to the entire data model."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "value": {\r\n'
              '              "description": "The data to be updated in the data model. If present, the value at \'path\' is replaced (or created). If omitted, the key at \'path\' is removed.",\r\n'
              '              "additionalProperties": true\r\n'
              '            }\r\n'
              '          },\r\n'

              '        },\r\n'

              '            "theme": {\n'

    Actual: 'You are a helpful assistant who chats with a user.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Your responses should contain acknowledgment of the user message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: When you are asking for information from the user, you should always include\r\n'
              'at least one submit button of some kind or another submitting element so that\r\n'
              'the user can indicate that they are done providing information.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Use the provided tools to respond to user using rich UI elements.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'The active catalog ID is: "test_catalog". You must use this catalog ID when creating surfaces.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to execute code. If you need to perform calculations, do them yourself.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to use tools for UI generation.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to use function calls for UI generation.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**REQUIRED PROPERTIES:** You MUST include ALL required properties for every component, even if they are inside a template or will be bound to data.\r\n'
              '- For \'Text\', you MUST provide \'text\'. If dynamic, use { "path": "..." }.\r\n'
              '- For \'Image\', you MUST provide \'url\'. If dynamic, use { "path": "..." }.\r\n'
              '- For \'Button\', you MUST provide \'action\'.\r\n'
              '- For \'TextField\', \'CheckBox\', etc., you MUST provide \'label\'.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**EXAMPLES:**\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '1. Create a surface:\r\n'
              '```json\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "version": "v0.9",\r\n'
              '  "createSurface": {\r\n'
              '    "surfaceId": "main",\r\n'
              '    "catalogId": "https://a2ui.org/specification/v0_9/catalogs/basic/catalog.json",\r\n'
              '    "sendDataModel": true\r\n'
              '  }\r\n'
              '}\r\n'
              '```\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '2. Update components:\r\n'
              '```json\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "version": "v0.9",\r\n'
              '  "updateComponents": {\r\n'
              '    "surfaceId": "main",\r\n'
              '    "components": [\r\n'
              '      {\r\n'
              '        // The root component MUST have id "root"\r\n'
              '        "id": "root",\r\n'
              '        "component": "Column",\r\n'
              '        "justify": "start",\r\n'
              '        "children": [\r\n'
              '          "headerText",\r\n'
              '          "content"\r\n'
              '        ]\r\n'
              '      }\r\n'
              '    ]\r\n'
              '  }\r\n'
              '}\r\n'
              '```\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**IMPORTANT:**\r\n'
              '- One of the components sent in one of the `updateComponents` MUST have id "root", or nothing will be displayed.\r\n'
              '- Do NOT nest `components` inside `createSurface`. Use `updateComponents` to add components to a surface.\r\n'
              '- `createSurface` ONLY sets up the surface (ID and catalog). It does NOT take content.\r\n'
              '- To show a UI, you typically send a `createSurface` message (if the surface doesn\'t exist), followed by an `updateComponents` message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Your responses should contain acknowledgment of the user message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: When you are asking for information from the user, you should always include\r\n'
              'at least one submit button of some kind or another submitting element so that\r\n'
              'the user can indicate that they are done providing information.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-----CONTROLLING_THE_UI_START-----\r\n'
              'You can control the UI by outputting valid A2UI JSON messages wrapped in markdown code blocks.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Supported messages are: `createSurface`, `updateComponents`, `deleteSurface`.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '- `createSurface`: Creates a new surface.\r\n'
              '- `updateComponents`: Updates components in a surface.\r\n'
              '- `deleteSurface`: Deletes a surface.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Properties:\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '- `createSurface`: Requires `surfaceId` (you must always use a unique ID for each created surface),\r\n'
              '`catalogId` (use the active catalog ID if provided in system instructions),\r\n'
              'and `sendDataModel: true`.\r\n'
              '- `updateComponents`: Requires `surfaceId` and a list of `components`.\r\n'
              'One component MUST have `id: "root"`.\r\n'
              '- `deleteSurface`: Requires `surfaceId`.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'To create a new UI:\r\n'
              '1. Output a `createSurface` message with a unique `surfaceId` and `catalogId` (use the active catalog ID if provided in system instructions).\r\n'
              '2. Output an `updateComponents` message with the `surfaceId` and the component definitions.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'To update an existing UI:\r\n'
              '1. Output an `updateComponents` message with the existing `surfaceId` and the new component definitions.\r\n'
              '-----CONTROLLING_THE_UI_END-----\r\n'

              '          "type": "number"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        {\n'
              '          "$ref": "#/$defs/DataBinding"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        {\n'
              '          "allOf": [\n'
              '            {\n'
              '              "$ref": "#/$defs/FunctionCall"\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            {\n'
              '              "properties": {\n'
              '                "returnType": {\n'
              '                  "const": "number"\n'
              '                }\n'
              '              }\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          ]\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      ]\n'
              '    },\n'
              '    "DynamicBoolean": {\n'

              '            {\n'

              '            {\n'

              '      ]\n'

              '{\n'
              '  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",\n'
              '  "$id": "https://a2ui.org/specification/v0_9/server_to_client.json",\n'
              '  "title": "A2UI Message Schema",\n'
              '  "description": "Describes a JSON payload for an A2UI (Agent to UI) message, which is used to dynamically construct and update user interfaces.",\n'
              '  "type": "object",\n'
              '  "oneOf": [\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/CreateSurfaceMessage"},\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateComponentsMessage"},\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateDataModelMessage"},\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/DeleteSurfaceMessage"}\n'
              '  ],\n'
              '  "$defs": {\n'
              '    "CreateSurfaceMessage": {\n'
              '      "type": "object",\n'
              '      "properties": {\n'
              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "createSurface": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Signals the client to create a new surface and begin rendering it. It is an error to send \'createSurface\' for a surfaceId that already exists without first deleting it. When this message is sent, the client will expect \'updateComponents\' and/or \'updateDataModel\' messages for the same surfaceId that define the component tree.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be rendered."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "catalogId": {\n'
              '              "description": "A string that uniquely identifies this catalog. It is recommended to prefix this with an internet domain that you own, to avoid conflicts e.g. mycompany.com:somecatalog\'.",\n'
              '              "type": "string"\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "theme": {\n'
              '              "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/theme",\n'
              '              "description": "Theme parameters for the surface (e.g., {\'primaryColor\': \'#FF0000\'}). These must validate against the \'theme\' schema defined in the catalog."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "sendDataModel": {\n'
              '              "type": "boolean",\n'
              '              "description": "If true, the client will send the full data model of this surface in the metadata of every A2A message sent to the server that created the surface. Defaults to false."\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "catalogId"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["createSurface", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    },\n'
              '    "UpdateComponentsMessage": {\n'
              '      "type": "object",\n'
              '      "properties": {\n'
              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "updateComponents": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Updates a surface with a new set of components. This message can be sent multiple times to update the component tree of an existing surface. One of the components in one of the components lists MUST have an \'id\' of \'root\' to serve as the root of the component tree. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be updated."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '\n'
              '            "components": {\n'
              '              "type": "array",\n'
              '              "description": "A list containing all UI components for the surface.",\n'
              '              "minItems": 1,\n'
              '              "items": {\n'
              '                "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/anyComponent"\n'
              '              }\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "components"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["updateComponents", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    },\n'
              '    "UpdateDataModelMessage": {\n'
              '      "type": "object",\n'
              '      "properties": {\n'
              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "updateDataModel": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Updates the data model for an existing surface. This message can be sent multiple times to update the data model. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface this data model update applies to."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "path": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "An optional path to a location within the data model (e.g., \'/user/name\'). If omitted, or set to \'/\', refers to the entire data model."\n'
              '            },\n'
              '            "value": {\n'
              '              "description": "The data to be updated in the data model. If present, the value at \'path\' is replaced (or created). If omitted, the key at \'path\' is removed.",\n'
              '              "additionalProperties": true\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["updateDataModel", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    },\n'
              '    "DeleteSurfaceMessage": {\n'
              '      "type": "object",\n'
              '      "properties": {\n'
              '        "version": {\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\n'
              '        },\n'
              '        "deleteSurface": {\n'
              '          "type": "object",\n'
              '          "description": "Signals the client to delete the surface identified by \'surfaceId\'. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\n'
              '          "properties": {\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\n'
              '              "type": "string",\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be deleted."\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          },\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId"],\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      "required": ["deleteSurface", "version"],\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '-----MESSAGE_SCHEMA_END-----\n'
              ''
    Actual: 'You are a helpful assistant who chats with a user.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Your responses should contain acknowledgment of the user message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: When you are asking for information from the user, you should always include\r\n'
              'at least one submit button of some kind or another submitting element so that\r\n'
              'the user can indicate that they are done providing information.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Use the provided tools to respond to user using rich UI elements.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'The active catalog ID is: "test_catalog". You must use this catalog ID when creating surfaces.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to execute code. If you need to perform calculations, do them yourself.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to use tools for UI generation.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: You do not have the ability to use function calls for UI generation.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**REQUIRED PROPERTIES:** You MUST include ALL required properties for every component, even if they are inside a template or will be bound to data.\r\n'
              '- For \'Text\', you MUST provide \'text\'. If dynamic, use { "path": "..." }.\r\n'
              '- For \'Image\', you MUST provide \'url\'. If dynamic, use { "path": "..." }.\r\n'
              '- For \'Button\', you MUST provide \'action\'.\r\n'
              '- For \'TextField\', \'CheckBox\', etc., you MUST provide \'label\'.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**EXAMPLES:**\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '1. Create a surface:\r\n'
              '```json\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "version": "v0.9",\r\n'
              '  "createSurface": {\r\n'
              '    "surfaceId": "main",\r\n'
              '    "catalogId": "https://a2ui.org/specification/v0_9/catalogs/basic/catalog.json",\r\n'
              '    "sendDataModel": true\r\n'
              '  }\r\n'
              '}\r\n'
              '```\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '2. Update components:\r\n'
              '```json\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "version": "v0.9",\r\n'
              '  "updateComponents": {\r\n'
              '    "surfaceId": "main",\r\n'
              '    "components": [\r\n'
              '      {\r\n'
              '        // The root component MUST have id "root"\r\n'
              '        "id": "root",\r\n'
              '        "component": "Column",\r\n'
              '        "justify": "start",\r\n'
              '        "children": [\r\n'
              '          "headerText",\r\n'
              '          "content"\r\n'
              '        ]\r\n'
              '      }\r\n'
              '    ]\r\n'
              '  }\r\n'
              '}\r\n'
              '```\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '**IMPORTANT:**\r\n'
              '- One of the components sent in one of the `updateComponents` MUST have id "root", or nothing will be displayed.\r\n'
              '- Do NOT nest `components` inside `createSurface`. Use `updateComponents` to add components to a surface.\r\n'
              '- `createSurface` ONLY sets up the surface (ID and catalog). It does NOT take content.\r\n'
              '- To show a UI, you typically send a `createSurface` message (if the surface doesn\'t exist), followed by an `updateComponents` message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Your responses should contain acknowledgment of the user message.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: When you are asking for information from the user, you should always include\r\n'
              'at least one submit button of some kind or another submitting element so that\r\n'
              'the user can indicate that they are done providing information.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-----CONTROLLING_THE_UI_START-----\r\n'
              'You can control the UI by outputting valid A2UI JSON messages wrapped in markdown code blocks.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Supported messages are: `createSurface`, `updateComponents`, `updateDataModel`.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '- `createSurface`: Creates a new surface.\r\n'
              '- `updateComponents`: Updates components in a surface.\r\n'
              '- `updateDataModel`: Updates the data model.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'Properties:\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '- `createSurface`: Requires `surfaceId` (you must always use a unique ID for each created surface),\r\n'
              '`catalogId` (use the active catalog ID if provided in system instructions),\r\n'
              'and `sendDataModel: true`.\r\n'
              '- `updateComponents`: Requires `surfaceId` and a list of `components`.\r\n'
              'One component MUST have `id: "root"`.\r\n'
              '- `updateDataModel`: Requires `surfaceId`, `path` and `value`.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'To create a new UI:\r\n'
              '1. Output a `createSurface` message with a unique `surfaceId` and `catalogId` (use the active catalog ID if provided in system instructions).\r\n'
              '2. Output an `updateComponents` message with the `surfaceId` and the component definitions.\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              'IMPORTANT: DO NOT update or modify surfaces created in previous turns. If the UI needs to change, you MUST create a NEW surface with a new unique `surfaceId`. You may only use `updateComponents` to populate the components of a freshly created surface.\r\n'
              '-----CONTROLLING_THE_UI_END-----\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-----OUTPUT_FORMAT_START-----\r\n'
              'When constructing UI, you must output a VALID A2UI JSON object representing one of the A2UI message types (`createSurface`, `updateComponents`, `updateDataModel`).\r\n'
              '- You can treat the A2UI schema as a specification for the JSON you typically output.\r\n'
              '- The JSON block must be valid and complete.\r\n'
              '- Ensure your JSON is fenced with ```json and ```.\r\n'
              '-----OUTPUT_FORMAT_END-----\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-------------------------------------\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '-----COMMON_TYPES_START-----\r\n'
              '{\r\n'
              '  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",\r\n'
              '  "$id": "common_types.json",\r\n'
              '  "title": "A2UI Common Types",\r\n'
              '  "description": "Common type definitions used across A2UI schemas.",\r\n'
              '  "$defs": {\r\n'
              '    "ComponentId": {\r\n'
              '      "type": "string",\r\n'
              '      "description": "The unique identifier for a component, used for both definitions and references within the same surface."\r\n'
              '    },\r\n'
              '    "AccessibilityAttributes": {\r\n'

              '{\r\n'
              '  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",\r\n'

              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/CreateSurfaceMessage"},\r\n'
              '    {"$ref": "#/$defs/UpdateComponentsMessage"},\r\n'

              '  ],\r\n'
              '  "$defs": {\r\n'
              '    "CreateSurfaceMessage": {\r\n'

              '      "properties": {\r\n'
              '        "version": {\r\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\r\n'
              '        },\r\n'
              '        "createSurface": {\r\n'
              '          "type": "object",\r\n'
              '          "description": "Signals the client to create a new surface and begin rendering it. It is an error to send \'createSurface\' for a surfaceId that already exists without first deleting it. When this message is sent, the client will expect \'updateComponents\' and/or \'updateDataModel\' messages for the same surfaceId that define the component tree.",\r\n'
              '          "properties": {\r\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "string",\r\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be rendered."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "catalogId": {\r\n'
              '              "description": "A string that uniquely identifies this catalog. It is recommended to prefix this with an internet domain that you own, to avoid conflicts e.g. mycompany.com:somecatalog\'.",\r\n'
              '              "type": "string"\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "theme": {\r\n'
              '              "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/theme",\r\n'
              '              "description": "Theme parameters for the surface (e.g., {\'primaryColor\': \'#FF0000\'}). These must validate against the \'theme\' schema defined in the catalog."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "sendDataModel": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "boolean",\r\n'
              '              "description": "If true, the client will send the full data model of this surface in the metadata of every A2A message sent to the server that created the surface. Defaults to false."\r\n'
              '            }\r\n'

              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "catalogId"],\r\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\r\n'

              '      "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '    },\r\n'
              '    "UpdateComponentsMessage": {\r\n'
              '      "type": "object",\r\n'

              '        "version": {\r\n'
              '          "const": "v0.9"\r\n'
              '        },\r\n'
              '        "updateComponents": {\r\n'
              '          "type": "object",\r\n'
              '          "description": "Updates a surface with a new set of components. This message can be sent multiple times to update the component tree of an existing surface. One of the components in one of the components lists MUST have an \'id\' of \'root\' to serve as the root of the component tree. The createSurface message MUST have been previously sent with the \'catalogId\' that is in this message.",\r\n'
              '          "properties": {\r\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "string",\r\n'
              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface to be updated."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '\r\n'
              '            "components": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "array",\r\n'
              '              "description": "A list containing all UI components for the surface.",\r\n'
              '              "minItems": 1,\r\n'
              '              "items": {\r\n'
              '                "$ref": "catalog.json#/$defs/anyComponent"\r\n'
              '              }\r\n'
              '            }\r\n'
              '          },\r\n'
              '          "required": ["surfaceId", "components"],\r\n'
              '          "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '        }\r\n'
              '      },\r\n'
              '      "required": ["updateComponents", "version"],\r\n'
              '      "additionalProperties": false\r\n'
              '    },\r\n'
              '    "UpdateDataModelMessage": {\r\n'

              '          "properties": {\r\n'
              '            "surfaceId": {\r\n'

              '              "description": "The unique identifier for the UI surface this data model update applies to."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "path": {\r\n'
              '              "type": "string",\r\n'
              '              "description": "An optional path to a location within the data model (e.g., \'/user/name\'). If omitted, or set to \'/\', refers to the entire data model."\r\n'
              '            },\r\n'
              '            "value": {\r\n'
              '              "description": "The data to be updated in the data model. If present, the value at \'path\' is replaced (or created). If omitted, the key at \'path\' is removed.",\r\n'
              '              "additionalProperties": true\r\n'
              '            }\r\n'
              '          },\r\n'
